The following actions target Node.js 20 but are being forced to run on Node.js 24: actions/checkout@v3, actions/setup-java@v3, actions/setup-python@v4, aws-actions/configure-aws-credentials@v3. For more information see: https://github.blog/changelog/2025-09-19-deprecation-of-node-20-on-github-actions-runners/
